Zone 0 · 4,809 arc traversals · 7.79 h driving + 0.75 h break = **8.54 h**

Capture 3.78 h · deadhead 2.37 h · turns 1.65 h


Start and finish at the depot. **Capture** rows are required footage: keep to 40 km/h or below unless the row says *reduced*. **Deadhead** rows are repositioning — drive them normally, the camera is not counted.


> **383 streets on this route are marked [HOLD 40 — still owed].** You would otherwise have driven past them; filming them today is what saves a return trip later in the campaign.
